Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The astrophysics variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 737 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 705 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_737(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 737. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^705(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)
